# ====================================================================
# SECTION: VIE_md_effects.txt

# Vietnam submod scripted effects (Phase 1, revised in Phase 3)

########################################################################
# Balance of power helpers: Conservative (-) <-> Reform (+)
# Guarded by VIE_bop_active so they are safe before init / after regime change.
########################################################################
VIE_bop_reform_small = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_bop_active } add_power_balance_value = { id = VIE_party_balance value = 0.05 } }
}
VIE_bop_reform_medium = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_bop_active } add_power_balance_value = { id = VIE_party_balance value = 0.10 } }
}
VIE_bop_reform_large = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_bop_active } add_power_balance_value = { id = VIE_party_balance value = 0.15 } }
}
VIE_bop_conservative_small = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_bop_active } add_power_balance_value = { id = VIE_party_balance value = -0.05 } }
}
VIE_bop_conservative_medium = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_bop_active } add_power_balance_value = { id = VIE_party_balance value = -0.10 } }
}
VIE_bop_conservative_large = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_bop_active } add_power_balance_value = { id = VIE_party_balance value = -0.15 } }
}

########################################################################
# South China Sea tension (0-3). Caller: set_temp_variable = { VIE_tension_add = N }
########################################################################
VIE_add_scs_tension = {
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { VIE_scs_tension = VIE_tension_add }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_scs_tension min = 0 max = 3 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIE_scs_tension > 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_scs_escalated }
	}
}

########################################################################
# Historical leaders (Party Congress chain). Fictional leaders for alt regimes live in
# VIE_political_leaders.txt (set_leader_VIE override). Names ASCII (HOI4 font).
########################################################################
# Every General Secretary has two effects:
#   VIE_create_leader_<x>  only creates the character (used by set_leader_VIE to rebuild the
#                          current leader when MD calls set_leader, which kills the leader first)
#   VIE_new_leader_<x>     Congress hand-over: kill, create, remember who holds the post (VIE_gs)
# VIE_gs: 1 Phieu, 2 Manh, 3 Trong, 4 Dung, 5 To Lam, 6 Congress XV, 7 Congress XVI
VIE_create_leader_phieu = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 4 } }
		create_country_leader = {
			name = VIE_leader_le_kha_phieu
			picture = "Le_Kha_Phieu.dds"
			ideology = Communist-State
			traits = { emerging_communist_state cautious stubborn }
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_country_leader = {
			name = VIE_leader_le_kha_phieu
			picture = "Le_Kha_Phieu.dds"
			ideology = Neutral_Communism
			traits = { neutrality_Neutral_Communism cautious }
		}
	}
}
VIE_create_leader_manh = {
	create_country_leader = {
		name = VIE_leader_nong_duc_manh
		picture = "Nong_Duc_Manh.dds"
		ideology = Neutral_Communism
		traits = { neutrality_Neutral_Communism polished }
	}
}
VIE_create_leader_trong = {
	create_country_leader = {
		name = VIE_leader_nguyen_phu_trong
		picture = "Nguyen_Phu_Trong.dds"
		ideology = Neutral_Communism
		traits = { writer neutrality_Neutral_Communism rational cautious }
	}
}
VIE_create_leader_dung = {
	create_country_leader = {
		name = VIE_leader_nguyen_tan_dung
		picture = "Nguyen_Tan_Dung.dds"
		ideology = Neutral_Communism
		traits = { neutrality_Neutral_Communism polished }
	}
}
VIE_create_leader_to_lam = {
	create_country_leader = {
		name = VIE_leader_to_lam
		picture = "To_Lam.dds"
		ideology = Neutral_Communism
		traits = { neutrality_Neutral_Communism cautious }
	}
}
VIE_new_leader_phieu = {
	hidden_effect = {
		kill_country_leader = yes
		set_country_flag = VIE_congress_controls_leader
		set_variable = { VIE_gs = 1 }
		VIE_create_leader_phieu = yes
	}
}
VIE_new_leader_manh = {
	hidden_effect = {
		kill_country_leader = yes
		set_country_flag = VIE_congress_controls_leader
		set_variable = { VIE_gs = 2 }
		VIE_create_leader_manh = yes
	}
}
VIE_new_leader_trong = {
	hidden_effect = {
		kill_country_leader = yes
		set_country_flag = VIE_congress_controls_leader
		set_variable = { VIE_gs = 3 }
		VIE_create_leader_trong = yes
	}
}
VIE_new_leader_dung = {
	hidden_effect = {
		kill_country_leader = yes
		set_country_flag = VIE_congress_controls_leader
		set_variable = { VIE_gs = 4 }
		VIE_create_leader_dung = yes
	}
}
# Fictional leader when the Party returns to power after another regime (no real person in alt paths)
VIE_create_leader_restored_party = {
	create_country_leader = {
		name = VIE_leader_restored_party
		ideology = Neutral_Communism
		traits = { neutrality_Neutral_Communism cautious }
	}
}

# Rebuild whoever holds the post (called from set_leader_VIE after MD's set_leader killed the leader)
VIE_recreate_general_secretary = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIE_gs = 1 } } VIE_create_leader_phieu = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIE_gs = 2 } } VIE_create_leader_manh = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIE_gs = 3 } } VIE_create_leader_trong = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIE_gs = 4 } } VIE_create_leader_dung = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIE_gs = 5 } } VIE_create_leader_to_lam = yes }
	else = { VIE_create_leader_restored_party = yes }
}
########################################################################
# Party popularity helper (MD change_relative_party_popularity). Adds AMOUNT to the outlook AND the party,
# so it also works for parties whose outlook has ~0 popularity.  Usage:
#   VIE_boost_party = { PARTY = 20 AMOUNT = 0.15 }
########################################################################
########################################################################
# Single gateway for regime changes.
# Caller: set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = <MD party index> }  VIE_transition_regime = yes
# Party indexes: 19 CPV(Neutral_Communism) 4 Communist-State 7 Autocracy (20 Nat_Populism and 21 Nat_Fascism are not used by the submod)
#                22 Nat_Autocracy 16 Neutral_Libertarian 15 oligarchism 13 Neutral_Autocracy
#                0 Western_Autocracy 1 conservatism 2 liberalism 3 socialism
# Idempotent: does nothing if the country already has that ruling party.
########################################################################
VIE_transition_regime = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { ruling_party = rul_party_temp } } }

		# The new ruling party must not be banned (VIE starts with all parties but the ruling one banned)
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = rul_party_temp }
		unban_party_scripted_call = yes

		# Elections only for the democratic parties (change_ruling_party_effect reads disable_elections)
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { rul_party_temp = 1 }
					check_variable = { rul_party_temp = 2 }
					check_variable = { rul_party_temp = 3 }
					check_variable = { rul_party_temp = 14 }
					check_variable = { rul_party_temp = 18 }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 0 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 } }

		# Drop every VIE balance of power before the ruling party changes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_bop_active }
			remove_power_balance = { id = VIE_party_balance }
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_bop_active }
		}

		# Leaving the hardline party (4): drop its ideas and the reform pressure while ruling_party is still 4
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 4 } }
			VIE_hl_cleanup = yes
		}

		set_temp_variable = { change_leader_temp = 1 }
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes   # MD: sets ruling_party, set_politics, runs set_leader_VIE

		# Balance of power of the new regime
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { ruling_party = 19 }
					check_variable = { ruling_party = 4 }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_party_rule_ended }
			set_power_balance = {
				id = VIE_party_balance
				left_side = VIE_conservative_side
				right_side = VIE_reform_side
				set_value = 0
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_bop_active }
		}
		else = {
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_party_rule_ended }
			# Party rule is over: set_leader_VIE must build the leader of a later return to slot 19 itself
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_congress_controls_leader }
			hidden_effect = { set_variable = { VIE_gs = 0 } }
			# Four Nos do not survive these regimes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 22 }
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 0 }
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_four_nos } remove_ideas = VIE_four_nos }
				if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_non_alignment_policy } remove_ideas = VIE_non_alignment_policy }
			}
		}

		# Blocks other doors for 6 months so two regime changes cannot stack
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_regime_changed_recently value = 1 days = 180 } }
		hidden_effect = { set_variable = { VIE_regime = ruling_party } }
	}
}

########################################################################
# Pop-up scheduler. Called monthly (see VIE_md_on_actions.txt).
#  Class A (turning point): always fires, sets the 45 day cooldown.
#  Class B (choice event):  fires only when the cooldown is free; if it is still blocked
#                           after DATE_TO the historical result is applied silently (FALLBACK).
# Catch-up mode (temp variable VIE_catch_up = 1): only sets the "already happened" flags, fires nothing.
# Used when a civil-war winner takes over the VIE lineage (see VIE_catch_up_schedule).
########################################################################
VIE_event_scheduler = {
	# One-time init (hidden event vie_pol.1)
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_md_init_done } }
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_md_init_done }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } } }
			country_event = { id = vie_pol.1 }
		}
	}

	# Party Congresses: only while the Party rules
	if = {
		limit = { VIE_party_rule_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				date > 2001.3.31
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_congress_9 }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_congress_9 }
			VIE_start_congress_term_9 = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
				# Khai mac Dai hoi IX: 19/04/2001; scheduler chay ngay 01/04.
				country_event = { id = vie_pol.2 days = 18 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				date > 2006.3.31
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_congress_10 }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_congress_10 }
			VIE_start_congress_term_10 = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
				# Khai mac Dai hoi X: 18/04/2006; scheduler chay ngay 01/04.
				country_event = { id = vie_pol.3 days = 17 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				date > 2010.12.31
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_congress_11 }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_congress_11 }
			VIE_start_congress_term_11 = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
				# Khai mac Dai hoi XI: 12/01/2011; scheduler chay ngay 01/01.
				country_event = { id = vie_pol.4 days = 11 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				date > 2015.12.31
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_congress_12 }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_congress_12 }
			VIE_start_congress_term_12 = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
				# Khai mac Dai hoi XII: 20/01/2016; scheduler chay ngay 01/01.
				country_event = { id = vie_pol.5 days = 19 }
			}
		}
	}

	# 2019 Defence White Paper: informal non-alignment becomes "Four Nos" (class C: no pop-up)
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 2019.10.31
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_four_nos }
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_four_nos }
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = VIE_non_alignment_policy }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = VIE_non_alignment_policy add_idea = VIE_four_nos }
		}
	}

	# Class B: Nam Cam case (2002-03)
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 2002.8.31
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_nam_cam }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_nam_cam }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd } }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_nam_cam }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
			country_event = { id = vie_eco.2 days = 3 random_days = 20 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { date > 2003.2.1 }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_nam_cam }
			VIE_fb_nam_cam = yes
		}
	}

	# Class A: HD-981 oil rig standoff (May 2014). Fires regardless of regime.
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 2014.4.30
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_hd981 }
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_hd981 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } } }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
			country_event = { id = vie_scs.1 days = 3 random_days = 15 }
		}
	}
}

# Silent historical outcome for the Nam Cam case when the pop-up window has passed
VIE_fb_nam_cam = {
	decrease_corruption = yes
}

# Chinh sach v32: focus co loi ich truc tiep; ban giao nang bac, khong cong chong.
# Ha tang v30: reward, giai ngan, ban giao va hang doi popup.

VIE_infra_infrastructure_development_reward = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_tt_infra_policy_infrastructure_development
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = VIE
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_infrastructure_development_adopted
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_infrastructure_development_adopted
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = VIE_ax_initialized
					}
				}
				VIE_ax_init = yes
				set_country_flag = VIE_ax_initialized
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_open_infrastructure_development
		}
		add_political_power = 25
		hidden_effect = {
			VIE_infra_refresh_policy_ideas = yes
		}
	}
}

VIE_infra_expressway_bot_reward = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_tt_infra_policy_expressway_bot
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = VIE
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_expressway_bot_adopted
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_funding_public
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_expressway_bot_adopted
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_open_expressway_bot
		}
		set_country_flag = VIE_infra_funding_bot
		add_to_variable = {
			VIE_ax_market = 1
			tooltip = VIE_ax_market_tt
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			temp_opinion = 3
		}
		change_industrial_conglomerates_opinion = yes
		VIE_ax_normalize = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			VIE_infra_refresh_policy_ideas = yes
		}
	}
}

		}

}

VIE_infra_d_road_foundation_1_start = {
	if = {
		limit = { VIE_infra_d_road_foundation_1_can_start = yes }
		set_variable = { VIE_infra_road_foundation_paid = 7 }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = VIE_infra_road_foundation_paid }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = VIE_infra_d_road_foundation_1_running
		set_country_flag = VIE_infra_road_foundation_running
		set_country_flag = VIE_infra_road_busy
	}
}

VIE_infra_d_road_foundation_1_finish = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIE_infra_d_road_foundation_1_running
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					VIE_infra_road_foundation_stage = 0
				}
				owns_state = 521
				controls_state = 521
				owns_state = 518
				controls_state = 518
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 1
			}
			521 = {
				VIE_infra_prepaid_infrastructure = yes
			}
			518 = {
				VIE_infra_prepaid_infrastructure = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 0
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				VIE_infra_road_foundation_stage = 1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				VIE_expressway_km = 400
				tooltip = VIE_expressway_km_tt
			}
			clamp_variable = {
				var = VIE_expressway_km
				min = 0
				max = 5000
			}
			VIE_infra_refresh_roads = yes
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_road_foundation_done
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_quality_pending
			VIE_infra_refresh_policy_ideas = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = {
				treasury_change = VIE_infra_road_foundation_paid
			}
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_tt_infra_refunded
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIE_infra_d_road_foundation_1_running
		clr_country_flag = VIE_infra_road_foundation_running
		clr_country_flag = VIE_infra_road_busy
		clear_variable = VIE_infra_road_foundation_paid
	}
}

			has_country_flag = VIE_infra_d_road_5000_1_running
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					VIE_infra_road_5000_stage = 0
				}
				owns_state = 520
				controls_state = 520
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 1
			}
			520 = {
				VIE_infra_prepaid_infrastructure = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 0
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				VIE_infra_road_5000_stage = 1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				VIE_expressway_km = 500
				tooltip = VIE_expressway_km_tt
			}
			clamp_variable = {
				var = VIE_expressway_km
				min = 0
				max = 5000
			}
			VIE_infra_refresh_roads = yes
			VIE_infra_refresh_policy_ideas = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = {
				treasury_change = VIE_infra_road_5000_paid
			}
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_tt_infra_refunded
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIE_infra_d_road_5000_1_running
		clr_country_flag = VIE_infra_road_5000_running
		clr_country_flag = VIE_infra_road_busy
		clear_variable = VIE_infra_road_5000_paid
	}
}

VIE_infra_d_road_5000_2_start = {
	if = {
		limit = { VIE_infra_d_road_5000_2_can_start = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_funding_bot } set_variable = { VIE_infra_road_5000_paid = 2.625 } }
		else = { set_variable = { VIE_infra_road_5000_paid = 3.5 } }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = VIE_infra_road_5000_paid }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = VIE_infra_d_road_5000_2_running
		set_country_flag = VIE_infra_road_5000_running
		set_country_flag = VIE_infra_road_busy
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_funding_bot NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_bot_resolved } NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_bot_queued } } set_country_flag = VIE_infra_bot_pending }
	}
}

		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = VIE_infra_d_rail_training_1_running
		set_country_flag = VIE_infra_rail_training_running
		set_country_flag = VIE_infra_rail_busy

}

VIE_infra_d_rail_training_1_finish = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIE_infra_d_rail_training_1_running
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					VIE_infra_rail_training_stage = 0
				}
				always = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 1
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 0
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				VIE_infra_rail_training_stage = 1
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_rail_training_done
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = VIE_hsr_partner_japan
				}
				add_tech_bonus = {
					name = VIE_infra_rail_training_bonus
					category = CAT_industry
					uses = 1
					bonus = 0.5
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = VIE_hsr_partner_eu
				}
				add_tech_bonus = {
					name = VIE_infra_rail_training_bonus
					category = CAT_industry
					uses = 1
					bonus = 0.4
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_tech_bonus = {
					name = VIE_infra_rail_training_bonus
					category = CAT_industry
					uses = 1
					bonus = 0.3
				}
			}
			VIE_infra_refresh_policy_ideas = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = {
				treasury_change = VIE_infra_rail_training_paid
			}
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_tt_infra_refunded
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIE_infra_d_rail_training_1_running
		clr_country_flag = VIE_infra_rail_training_running
		clr_country_flag = VIE_infra_rail_busy
		clear_variable = VIE_infra_rail_training_paid
	}
}

VIE_infra_refresh_roads = {
	VIE_infra_policy_refresh_road = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				VIE_expressway_km > 2999
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_3000_queued
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_3000_resolved
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = VIE_infra_3000_pending
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				VIE_expressway_km > 4999
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_5000_queued
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_5000_resolved
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = VIE_infra_5000_pending
	}
}

VIE_event_scheduler_infra = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = VIE_railway_national_program
				date > 2010.4.30
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_vote_resolved }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_vote_queued }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd }
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_vote_queued
			set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = vie_infra.4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_partner_pending
				VIE_infra_partner_unselected = yes
				OR = { country_exists = JAP country_exists = CHI country_exists = GER country_exists = FRA }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_partner_resolved }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_partner_queued }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd }
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_partner_queued
			set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = vie_infra.5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_quality_pending
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_quality_resolved }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_quality_queued }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd }
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_quality_queued
			set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = vie_infra.1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_bot_pending
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_bot_resolved }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_bot_queued }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd }
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_bot_queued
			set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = vie_infra.2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_3000_pending
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_3000_resolved }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_3000_queued }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd }
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_3000_queued
			set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = vie_infra.6 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_5000_pending
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_5000_resolved }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_5000_queued }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd }
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_5000_queued
			set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = vie_infra.7 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_long_thanh_notice_pending
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_long_thanh_notice_resolved }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_long_thanh_notice_queued }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd }
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_long_thanh_notice_queued
			set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = vie_infra.8 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_hsr_notice_pending
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_hsr_notice_resolved }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_infra_hsr_notice_queued }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd }
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_infra_hsr_notice_queued
			set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = vie_infra.9 }
		}
	}
	if = {
			limit = {
				date > 2020.3.31
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_vna_covid }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_vna_covid }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_vna_covid }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
				country_event = { id = vie_infra.3 days = 3 random_days = 20 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { date > 2020.9.30 }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_vna_covid }
				VIE_fb_none = yes
			}
		}
}

VIE_infra_refresh_policy_ideas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			count_triggers = {
				amount = 5
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_railway_national_program_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_reunification_line_upgrade_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_urban_rail_program_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_north_south_hsr_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_rail_technology_transfer_adopted
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			VIE_infra_policy_rail_rank = 5
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			count_triggers = {
				amount = 4
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_railway_national_program_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_reunification_line_upgrade_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_urban_rail_program_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_north_south_hsr_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_rail_technology_transfer_adopted
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			VIE_infra_policy_rail_rank = 4
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			count_triggers = {
				amount = 3
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_railway_national_program_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_reunification_line_upgrade_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_urban_rail_program_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_north_south_hsr_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_rail_technology_transfer_adopted
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			VIE_infra_policy_rail_rank = 3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			count_triggers = {
				amount = 2
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_railway_national_program_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_reunification_line_upgrade_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_urban_rail_program_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_north_south_hsr_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_rail_technology_transfer_adopted
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			VIE_infra_policy_rail_rank = 2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			count_triggers = {
				amount = 1
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_railway_national_program_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_reunification_line_upgrade_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_urban_rail_program_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_north_south_hsr_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_rail_technology_transfer_adopted
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			VIE_infra_policy_rail_rank = 1
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = {
			VIE_infra_policy_rail_rank = 0
		}
	}
	VIE_infra_policy_refresh_road = yes
	VIE_infra_policy_refresh_rail = yes
	VIE_infra_policy_refresh_air = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_policy_multimodal_transport_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_infra_multimodal_done
				has_idea = VIE_infra_policy_multimodal_1_idea
				has_idea = VIE_multimodal_idea
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = VIE_infra_policy_ports_1_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_infra_policy_ports_1_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = VIE_ports_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_ports_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = VIE_infra_policy_logistics_1_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_infra_policy_logistics_1_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = VIE_logistics_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_logistics_idea
		}
		VIE_infra_policy_refresh_multimodal = yes
	}
	else = {
		VIE_infra_policy_refresh_ports = yes
		VIE_infra_policy_refresh_logistics = yes
	}
}

# Cong nghiep v31: reward, giai ngan, ban giao va hang doi.

VIE_ind_industrialization_strategy_reward = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_tt_ind_policy_industrialization_strategy
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = VIE
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_industrialization_strategy_adopted
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_industrialization_strategy_adopted
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = VIE_ind_strategy_rewarded
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = VIE_ax_initialized
					}
				}
				VIE_ax_init = yes
				set_country_flag = VIE_ax_initialized
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				VIE_ind_localization = 10
				tooltip = VIE_ind_localization_tt
			}
			add_political_power = 25
			set_country_flag = VIE_ind_strategy_rewarded
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			VIE_ind_refresh_policy_ideas = yes
		}
	}
}

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = VIE_ind_priority_design
						has_country_flag = VIE_ind_priority_fab
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_ind_priority_fab
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			VIE_ind_refresh_policy_ideas = yes
		}
	}
}

VIE_ind_prepaid_industrial_complex = {
	if = { limit = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = yes } } one_state_industrial_complex = yes }
	else = { custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_tt_ind_existing_capacity }
}

VIE_ind_prepaid_dockyard = {
	if = { limit = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT is_coastal = yes free_building_slots = { building = dockyard size > 0 include_locked = yes } } one_state_dockyard = yes }
	else = { custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_tt_ind_existing_capacity }
}

VIE_ind_d_support_start = {
	if = { limit = { VIE_ind_d_support_can_start = yes }
		set_variable = { VIE_ind_support_paid = 2 }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = VIE_ind_support_paid } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 } modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = VIE_ind_d_support_running set_country_flag = VIE_ind_support_running set_country_flag = VIE_ind_common_busy
	}
}

VIE_ind_d_support_finish = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIE_ind_d_support_running
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					VIE_ind_support_stage = 0
				}
				always = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				VIE_ind_support_stage = 1
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_ind_support_done
			add_to_variable = {
				VIE_ind_localization = 6
				tooltip = VIE_ind_localization_tt
			}
			VIE_ind_refresh_policy_ideas = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = {
				treasury_change = VIE_ind_support_paid
			}
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_tt_ind_refunded
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIE_ind_d_support_running
		clr_country_flag = VIE_ind_support_running
		clr_country_flag = VIE_ind_common_busy
		clear_variable = VIE_ind_support_paid
	}
}

VIE_ind_d_ship_governance_start = {
	if = { limit = { VIE_ind_d_ship_governance_can_start = yes }
		set_variable = { VIE_ind_ship_governance_paid = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = VIE_ind_ship_governance_paid } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 } modify_treasury_effect = yes

VIE_ind_d_ship_governance_finish = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIE_ind_d_ship_governance_running
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					VIE_ind_ship_governance_stage = 0
				}
				always = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				VIE_ind_ship_governance_stage = 1
			}
			set_country_flag = VIE_ind_ship_governance_done
			add_to_variable = {
				VIE_ind_localization = 2
				tooltip = VIE_ind_localization_tt
			}
			set_variable = {
				VIE_vinashin_risk = 0
			}
			add_stability = 0.01
			add_to_variable = {
				VIE_ax_checks = 1
				tooltip = VIE_ax_checks_tt
			}
			VIE_ax_normalize = yes
			VIE_ind_refresh_policy_ideas = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = {
				treasury_change = VIE_ind_ship_governance_paid
			}
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_tt_ind_refunded
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIE_ind_d_ship_governance_running
		clr_country_flag = VIE_ind_ship_governance_running
		clr_country_flag = VIE_ind_ship_busy
		clear_variable = VIE_ind_ship_governance_paid
	}
}

VIE_event_scheduler_industry = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_steel_choice_pending has_completed_focus = VIE_national_steel_program NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_steel_selected } NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_steel_choice_queued } NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd } } set_country_flag = VIE_ind_steel_choice_queued set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } country_event = { id = vie_ind.1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_origin_pending has_completed_focus = VIE_manufacturing_hub has_country_flag = VIE_fdi_open_door NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_origin_resolved } NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_origin_queued } NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd } } set_country_flag = VIE_ind_origin_queued set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } country_event = { id = vie_ind.2 } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_fab_notice_pending has_country_flag = VIE_ind_chip_fab_done NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_fab_notice_resolved } NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_fab_notice_queued } NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd } } set_country_flag = VIE_ind_fab_notice_queued set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } country_event = { id = vie_ind.3 } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_auto_notice_pending has_country_flag = VIE_ind_auto_export_done NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_auto_notice_resolved } NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_auto_notice_queued } NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd } } set_country_flag = VIE_ind_auto_notice_queued set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } country_event = { id = vie_auto.1 } }
	}
}

# Chinh sach v32: bac idea thay the, khong cong chong.
VIE_ind_policy_refresh_support = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_support_done
				has_idea = VIE_ind_supporting_capacity_idea
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = VIE_ind_policy_support_1_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_ind_policy_support_1_idea
		}
		if = {

			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_ind_policy_chip_6_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = VIE_chip_sector_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_chip_sector_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_idea = VIE_ind_policy_chip_2_idea
				}
			}
			add_ideas = VIE_ind_policy_chip_2_idea
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = {
					VIE_ind_policy_chip_rank > 0
				}
				has_idea = VIE_ind_policy_chip_1_idea
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = VIE_ind_policy_chip_2_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_ind_policy_chip_2_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = VIE_ind_policy_chip_3_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_ind_policy_chip_3_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = VIE_ind_policy_chip_4_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_ind_policy_chip_4_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = VIE_semiconductor_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_semiconductor_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = VIE_ind_policy_chip_6_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_ind_policy_chip_6_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = VIE_chip_sector_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = VIE_chip_sector_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_idea = VIE_ind_policy_chip_1_idea
				}
			}
			add_ideas = VIE_ind_policy_chip_1_idea
		}
	}
}

VIE_ind_refresh_policy_ideas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			count_triggers = {
				amount = 4
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_engineers_adopted
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_design_packaging_priority_adopted
					has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_pilot_fab_priority_adopted
				}
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_design_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_osat_packaging_adopted
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			VIE_ind_policy_chip_rank = 4
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			count_triggers = {
				amount = 3
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_engineers_adopted
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_design_packaging_priority_adopted
					has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_pilot_fab_priority_adopted
				}
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_design_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_osat_packaging_adopted
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			VIE_ind_policy_chip_rank = 3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			count_triggers = {
				amount = 2
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_engineers_adopted
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_design_packaging_priority_adopted
					has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_pilot_fab_priority_adopted
				}
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_design_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_osat_packaging_adopted
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			VIE_ind_policy_chip_rank = 2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			count_triggers = {
				amount = 1
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_engineers_adopted
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_design_packaging_priority_adopted
					has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_pilot_fab_priority_adopted
				}
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_chip_design_adopted
				has_country_flag = VIE_ind_policy_osat_packaging_adopted
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			VIE_ind_policy_chip_rank = 1
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = {
			VIE_ind_policy_chip_rank = 0
		}
	}
	VIE_ind_policy_refresh_support = yes
	VIE_ind_policy_refresh_steel = yes
	VIE_ind_policy_refresh_ship = yes
	VIE_ind_policy_refresh_textile = yes
	VIE_ind_policy_refresh_ev = yes
	VIE_ind_policy_refresh_auto_export = yes
	VIE_ind_policy_refresh_electronics = yes
	VIE_ind_policy_refresh_chip = yes
	VIE_ind_policy_refresh_productivity = yes
}

# OVERRIDE of Millennium Dawn common/scripted_effects/VIE_political_leaders.txt
# Same file name on purpose: this submod loads after MD, so this copy wins.
# Changes vs MD: every alt-regime leader is fictional (no living/real figures);
# Party 19 (historical CPV): while VIE_congress_controls_leader is set the current General Secretary
# is rebuilt (MD's set_leader kills the leader before calling this); otherwise a fictional
# restored-Party leader is created.
# Each regime has one leader, so the MD per-party counters are reset first: every call creates the
# regime leader again instead of falling through to a random generated leader.
# TODO: diff against the MD original whenever MD updates.
set_leader_VIE = {
	set_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { socialism_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { Communist-State_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { oligarchism_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { Neutral_Libertarian_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { anarchist_communism_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { neutral_Social_leader = 0 }
	set_variable = { Neutral_Communism_leader = 0 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Vo Quang Thai"
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Dang Huu Phuoc"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Ly Thanh Hai"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 3 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Bui Ngoc Lan"
				ideology = socialism
				traits = {
					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 4 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Communist-State_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Communist-State_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_congress9_phieu }
				create_country_leader = {
					name = VIE_leader_le_kha_phieu
					picture = "Le_Kha_Phieu.dds"
					ideology = Communist-State
					traits = {
						emerging_communist_state
						cautious
						stubborn
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				create_country_leader = {
					name = "Pham Dinh Tuan"
					ideology = Communist-State
					traits = {
						emerging_communist_state
					}
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Communist-State_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 7 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

		}

# Party Congress spine (see VIE_congress_spine_redesign.md).
# Sets the term variable and drops whichever term-spirit idea the previous Congress granted.

VIE_clear_congress_resolution_ideas = {
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_resolution_9_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_resolution_9_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_resolution_9_cons_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_resolution_9_cons_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_resolution_10_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_resolution_10_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_resolution_11_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_resolution_11_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_resolution_12_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_resolution_12_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_resolution_12_dev_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_resolution_12_dev_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_resolution_13_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_resolution_13_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_resolution_14_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_resolution_14_idea }
}

VIE_start_congress_term_9 = {
	set_variable = { VIE_congress_term = 9 }

# ====================================================================
# SECTION: VIE_md_effects_axis.txt
# ====================================================================

# Scripted effects for Vietnam State-Building.
# Nine hidden axis variables (VIE_ax_<x>, written by ~370 focuses) are normalized to -10..+10, then folded
# every month into FOUR visible axes (see VIE_statebuilding_4axis_design.md):
#   A Nang luc Bo may       VIE_sb_A = merit_norm - size_norm/2
#   B Khong gian Chinh tri  VIE_sb_B = civil_norm + (checks_norm - mob_norm)/2
#   C Mo hinh Kinh te       VIE_sb_C = market_norm + decent_norm/2
#   D Dinh huong Doi ngoai  VIE_sb_D = integ_norm + west_norm/2
# The four visible axes are a pure function of the nine hidden axes (the state-building decisions and their
# offsets were removed). Modifier, factions and gates read A..D only.

VIE_ax_init = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIE_state_modifier } }
			}
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIE_state_modifier }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_ax_initialized }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_ax_initialized }
			# Khoi tao bien truc an mot lan; reward focus tiep tuc thay doi cac bien nay sau khi bat dau game.
			set_variable = { VIE_ax_size = 0 }
			set_variable = { VIE_ax_merit = 0 }
			set_variable = { VIE_ax_decent = 0 }
			set_variable = { VIE_ax_checks = 0 }
			set_variable = { VIE_ax_market = 0 }
			set_variable = { VIE_ax_civil = 0 }
			set_variable = { VIE_ax_integ = 0 }
			set_variable = { VIE_ax_mob = 0 }
			set_variable = { VIE_ax_west = 0 }
			VIE_ax_normalize = yes
		}
	}
}

VIE_ax_normalize = {
	hidden_effect = {
		# Recursion guard: set flag FIRST before calling VIE_ax_init to prevent
		# mutual infinite recursion (VIE_ax_normalize -> VIE_ax_init -> VIE_ax_normalize -> ...).
		# VIE_ax_init checks this flag and skips its body if already set.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_ax_initialized } }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_ax_initialized }
			VIE_ax_init = yes
		}

		# 1. Size (SPAN 12)
		set_variable = { VIE_ax_size_norm = VIE_ax_size }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_ax_size_norm value = 10 }
		divide_variable = { var = VIE_ax_size_norm value = 12 }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_ax_size_norm min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_ax_size_norm

		# 2. Merit (SPAN 59)
		set_variable = { VIE_ax_merit_norm = VIE_ax_merit }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_ax_merit_norm value = 10 }
		divide_variable = { var = VIE_ax_merit_norm value = 59 }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_ax_merit_norm min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_ax_merit_norm

		# 3. Decent (SPAN 24)
		set_variable = { VIE_ax_decent_norm = VIE_ax_decent }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_ax_decent_norm value = 10 }
		divide_variable = { var = VIE_ax_decent_norm value = 24 }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_ax_decent_norm min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_ax_decent_norm

		# 4. Checks (SPAN 19)
		set_variable = { VIE_ax_checks_norm = VIE_ax_checks }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_ax_checks_norm value = 10 }
		divide_variable = { var = VIE_ax_checks_norm value = 19 }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_ax_checks_norm min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_ax_checks_norm

		# 5. Market (SPAN 41)
		set_variable = { VIE_ax_market_norm = VIE_ax_market }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_ax_market_norm value = 10 }
		divide_variable = { var = VIE_ax_market_norm value = 41 }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_ax_market_norm min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_ax_market_norm

		# 6. Civil (SPAN 14)
		set_variable = { VIE_ax_civil_norm = VIE_ax_civil }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_ax_civil_norm value = 10 }
		divide_variable = { var = VIE_ax_civil_norm value = 14 }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_ax_civil_norm min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_ax_civil_norm

		# 7. Integ (SPAN 98)
		set_variable = { VIE_ax_integ_norm = VIE_ax_integ }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_ax_integ_norm value = 10 }
		divide_variable = { var = VIE_ax_integ_norm value = 98 }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_ax_integ_norm min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_ax_integ_norm

		# 8. Mob (SPAN 12)
		set_variable = { VIE_ax_mob_norm = VIE_ax_mob }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_ax_mob_norm value = 10 }
		divide_variable = { var = VIE_ax_mob_norm value = 12 }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_ax_mob_norm min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_ax_mob_norm

		# 9. West (SPAN 29)
		set_variable = { VIE_ax_west_norm = VIE_ax_west }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_ax_west_norm value = 10 }
		divide_variable = { var = VIE_ax_west_norm value = 29 }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_ax_west_norm min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_ax_west_norm

		# ---- Fold the nine hidden axes into the four visible ones ----
		# A = merit - size/2
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_A = VIE_ax_merit_norm }
		set_temp_variable = { sb_half = VIE_ax_size_norm }
		divide_variable = { var = sb_half value = 2 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = VIE_sb_A value = sb_half }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_sb_A min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_sb_A

		# B = civil + (checks - mob)/2
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_B = VIE_ax_civil_norm }
		set_temp_variable = { sb_half = VIE_ax_checks_norm }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = sb_half value = VIE_ax_mob_norm }
		divide_variable = { var = sb_half value = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VIE_sb_B value = sb_half }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_sb_B min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_sb_B

		# C = market + decent/2
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_C = VIE_ax_market_norm }
		set_temp_variable = { sb_half = VIE_ax_decent_norm }
		divide_variable = { var = sb_half value = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VIE_sb_C value = sb_half }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_sb_C min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_sb_C

		# D = integ + west/2
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_D = VIE_ax_integ_norm }
		set_temp_variable = { sb_half = VIE_ax_west_norm }
		divide_variable = { var = sb_half value = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VIE_sb_D value = sb_half }
		clamp_variable = { var = VIE_sb_D min = -10 max = 10 }
		round_variable = VIE_sb_D

		# Zone level per axis: 0 in -1..+1, +-0.5 in 2..5, +-1 from 6 (half / full pole effect)
		# Zone level of axis A: 0 in -1..+1, +-0.5 in 2..5, +-1 from 6
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_A_lv = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_A > 1 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_A_lv = 0.5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_A > 5 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_A_lv = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_A < -1 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_A_lv = -0.5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_A < -5 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_A_lv = -1 }
		}
		# Zone level of axis B: 0 in -1..+1, +-0.5 in 2..5, +-1 from 6
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_B_lv = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_B > 1 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_B_lv = 0.5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_B > 5 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_B_lv = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_B < -1 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_B_lv = -0.5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_B < -5 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_B_lv = -1 }
		}
		# Zone level of axis C: 0 in -1..+1, +-0.5 in 2..5, +-1 from 6
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_C_lv = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_C > 1 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_C_lv = 0.5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_C > 5 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_C_lv = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_C < -1 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_C_lv = -0.5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_C < -5 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_C_lv = -1 }
		}
		# Zone level of axis D: 0 in -1..+1, +-0.5 in 2..5, +-1 from 6
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_D_lv = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_D > 1 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_D_lv = 0.5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_D > 5 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_D_lv = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_D < -1 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_D_lv = -0.5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_sb_D < -5 } }
			set_variable = { VIE_sb_D_lv = -1 }
		}

		# Modifier variables, each linear in the zone level (left pole = negative level)
		# A: left = patronage (+PP, -efficiency); right = professional (-PP, +efficiency)
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_pp_mod = VIE_sb_A_lv }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_sb_pp_mod value = -0.06 }
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_eff_mod = VIE_sb_A_lv }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_sb_eff_mod value = 0.05 }
		set_temp_variable = { sb_t = VIE_sb_D_lv }
		multiply_variable = { var = sb_t value = -0.02 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VIE_sb_eff_mod value = sb_t }

		# B: left = security (+stability, +war support, -research); right = open (-stability, +research)
		# C: left = state-led (+military industry, +stability); right = market (+construction, +productivity, -stability)
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_stab_mod = VIE_sb_B_lv }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_sb_stab_mod value = -0.08 }
		set_temp_variable = { sb_t = VIE_sb_C_lv }
		multiply_variable = { var = sb_t value = -0.04 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VIE_sb_stab_mod value = sb_t }
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_war_mod = VIE_sb_B_lv }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_sb_war_mod value = -0.05 }
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_res_mod = VIE_sb_B_lv }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_sb_res_mod value = 0.05 }
		set_temp_variable = { sb_t = VIE_sb_D_lv }
		multiply_variable = { var = sb_t value = 0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VIE_sb_res_mod value = sb_t }
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_mil_mod = VIE_sb_C_lv }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_sb_mil_mod value = -0.08 }
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_build_mod = VIE_sb_C_lv }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_sb_build_mod value = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_prod_mod = VIE_sb_C_lv }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_sb_prod_mod value = 0.03 }

		# D: left = self-reliant (+efficiency, see above); right = integrated (+trade opinion, +research)
		set_variable = { VIE_sb_trade_mod = VIE_sb_D_lv }
		multiply_variable = { var = VIE_sb_trade_mod value = 0.05 }

		VIE_ax_faction_check = yes
		VIE_ax_events_check = yes
	}
}

# Evolution of internal factions based on the four state-building axes and regime flags.
# Respects Millennium Dawn's exact 3-slot limit for internal factions.
VIE_ax_faction_check = {
	# 1. Oligarchs: market runs ahead of the apparatus (C high, A low) under heavy corruption
	if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { VIE_sb_C > 3 }
				check_variable = { VIE_sb_A < 2 }
				OR = {
					has_idea = corruption_level_07
					has_idea = corruption_level_08
					has_idea = corruption_level_09
					has_idea = corruption_level_10
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_idea = oligarchs }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = industrial_conglomerates }
			remove_ideas = industrial_conglomerates
			add_ideas = oligarchs
		}
	}

	# 2. Intelligence Community: security primacy (B deep on the control side) or security regime active
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_security_active
				check_variable = { VIE_sb_B < -5 }
			}
			NOT = { has_idea = intelligence_community }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = farmers }
			remove_ideas = farmers
			add_ideas = intelligence_community
		}
	}

	# 3. The Military: control state plus a closed external posture
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIE_sb_B < -5 }
			check_variable = { VIE_sb_D < -3 }
			NOT = { has_idea = the_military }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = farmers }
			remove_ideas = farmers
			add_ideas = the_military
		}
	}

	# 4. Labour Unions: open political space
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIE_sb_B > 4 }
			NOT = { has_idea = labour_unions }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = farmers }
			remove_ideas = farmers
			add_ideas = labour_unions
		}
	}

	# 5. Small & Medium Business Owners: clean market development (C and A both high)
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIE_sb_C > 2 }
			check_variable = { VIE_sb_A > 2 }
			NOT = { has_idea = small_medium_business_owners }
			NOT = { has_idea = labour_unions }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = farmers }
			remove_ideas = farmers
			add_ideas = small_medium_business_owners
		}
	}
}

# Failure and reversal event triggers
VIE_ax_events_check = {
	# Multi-dimensional crisis warning: unstable, and either deep repression or a rotten apparatus
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_axis_4_fired }
			has_stability < 0.20
			OR = {
				check_variable = { VIE_sb_B < -5 }
				check_variable = { VIE_sb_A < -3 }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_axis_4_fired }
		country_event = { id = vie_axis.4 days = 10 }
	}
}

# ====================================================================
# SECTION: VIE_md_effects_hardline.txt
# ====================================================================

# Vietnam submod scripted effects: "Con duong Kien dinh" (CPV Hardline, ruling party 4).
# Reform pressure (VIE_rp, 0-100) is the only new variable of the branch:
#   0-30 stable | 31-55 simmering | 56-79 tense (idea) | 80-100 crisis (idea, forced event)
# Callers add pressure through a temp variable:
#   set_temp_variable = { VIE_rp_add = 6 }  VIE_hl_add_pressure = yes
# No parametrized effects here, so file load order does not matter.

# Adds VIE_rp_add (temp, may be negative) to the reform pressure. A rise starts the 120 day "recently tightened"
# window during which the monthly decay is paused.
VIE_hl_add_pressure = {
	hidden_effect = { VIE_hl_add_pressure_inner = yes }
}
VIE_hl_add_pressure_inner = {
	add_to_variable = { var = VIE_rp value = VIE_rp_add }
	clamp_variable = { var = VIE_rp min = 0 max = 100 }
	# During the self-reliance period (event vie_hl.7, "persist") the pressure stops at 80
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = VIE_hl_self_reliance_idea
			check_variable = { VIE_rp > 80 }
		}
		set_variable = { VIE_rp = 80 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIE_rp_add > 0 } }
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_hl_rp_recent value = 1 days = 120 } }
	}
	VIE_hl_update_pressure_idea = yes
}

# Keeps exactly the idea that matches the current level: none up to 55, tense 56-79, crisis from 80.
VIE_hl_update_pressure_idea = {
	hidden_effect = { VIE_hl_update_pressure_idea_inner = yes }
}
VIE_hl_update_pressure_idea_inner = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIE_rp > 79 } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_pressure_tense_idea }
			remove_ideas = VIE_hl_pressure_tense_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_idea = VIE_hl_pressure_crisis_idea } }
			add_ideas = VIE_hl_pressure_crisis_idea
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIE_rp > 55 } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_pressure_crisis_idea }
			remove_ideas = VIE_hl_pressure_crisis_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_idea = VIE_hl_pressure_tense_idea } }
			add_ideas = VIE_hl_pressure_tense_idea
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_pressure_tense_idea }
			remove_ideas = VIE_hl_pressure_tense_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_pressure_crisis_idea }
			remove_ideas = VIE_hl_pressure_crisis_idea
		}
	}
}

# Monthly tick (on_monthly, see VIE_md_on_actions.txt). Pressure eases by 1 a month when no tightening focus
# was finished in the last 120 days. Reaction events are added to this effect in phase 4.
VIE_hl_monthly = {
	if = {
		limit = { VIE_hl_in_power = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_hl_rp_recent }
				check_variable = { VIE_rp > 0 }
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { VIE_rp = 1 }
		}
		VIE_hl_update_pressure_idea = yes
		VIE_hl_event_scheduler = yes
	}
}

# Reaction events vie_hl.2 - .8 (see events/VIE_md_hardline.txt). Soft events respect the shared 45 day pop-up
# cooldown (VIE_popup_cd) and fire once; the crisis (.7) and the re-evaluation (.8) are forced.
VIE_hl_event_scheduler = {
	# .7 legitimacy crisis: pressure 80+, forced (ignores the cooldown), not while already self-reliant
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIE_rp > 79 }
			NOT = { has_idea = VIE_hl_self_reliance_idea }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev7_pending }
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_hl_ev7_pending value = 1 days = 60 } }
		country_event = { id = vie_hl.7 days = 2 }
	}
	# .8 re-evaluation: the self-reliance idea has run out
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIE_hl_self_reliance_running
			NOT = { has_idea = VIE_hl_self_reliance_idea }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev8_pending }
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_hl_ev8_pending value = 1 days = 60 } }
		country_event = { id = vie_hl.8 days = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd } }
		# .2 letter from the veterans
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { VIE_rp > 30 }
				has_completed_focus = VIE_hl_party_rectification
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev2_done }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev2_done }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
			country_event = { id = vie_hl.2 days = 3 random_days = 15 }
		}
		# .3 investors hold back
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { VIE_rp > 39 }
				has_completed_focus = VIE_hl_selective_fdi
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev3_done }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev3_done }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
			country_event = { id = vie_hl.3 days = 3 random_days = 15 }
		}
		# .4 labour and trade commitments (EVFTA / CPTPP)
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_completed_focus = VIE_evfta
					has_completed_focus = VIE_cptpp_member
				}
				has_completed_focus = VIE_hl_cyber_ideology
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev4_done }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev4_done }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
			country_event = { id = vie_hl.4 days = 3 random_days = 15 }
		}
		# .5 extraordinary Central Committee meeting: first time pressure reaches 56 (also fired by focus E1)
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { VIE_rp > 55 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev5_cd }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_hl_ev5_cd value = 1 days = 730 } }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
			country_event = { id = vie_hl.5 days = 3 random_days = 15 }
		}
		# .6 public reaction over the South China Sea
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				VIE_scs_escalated_trigger = yes
				has_completed_focus = VIE_hl_party_diplomacy
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev6_done }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev6_done }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
			country_event = { id = vie_hl.6 days = 3 random_days = 15 }
		}
	}
}

# Old integration focuses (evfta, cptpp_member, investment_grade, international_financial_centre) act as a
# relief valve while the hardline party rules: pressure -4, and the cadres resent it until the "socialist
# industrialization" tier legitimises opening up. Safe to call in any regime (does nothing outside party 4).
VIE_hl_integration_valve = {
	if = {
		limit = { VIE_hl_in_power = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { VIE_rp_add = -4 }
		VIE_hl_add_pressure = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_hl_rp_m4_tt
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = VIE_hl_five_year_plan } }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -2 }
			change_communist_cadres_opinion = yes
			VIE_bop_reform_small = yes
		}
	}
}

# Hardline takes power (called by event vie_hl.1). Same shape as the transition in VIE_sec_cyber_control:
# raise the party's popularity, then go through the single regime gateway. Idempotent.
# VIE_transition_regime resets the balance of power to 0, so it is moved back to -0.5 afterwards.
VIE_hl_take_power = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 4 } } }
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 4 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.15 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.15 }
		hidden_effect = { change_relative_party_popularity = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 4 }
		VIE_transition_regime = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 4 } }
			# The historical General Secretaries do not carry over into the hardline era
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_congress_controls_leader }
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_trong_third_term }
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_trong_third_term_confirmed }
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_to_lam_era }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_congress9_phieu }
				set_variable = { VIE_gs = 1 }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { VIE_gs = 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_bop_active }
				add_power_balance_value = { id = VIE_party_balance value = -0.5 }
			}
			set_variable = { VIE_rp = 20 }
			VIE_hl_update_pressure_idea = yes
		}
	}
}

# Called by VIE_transition_regime when the country leaves ruling party 4 (hooked in phase 2). Drops the branch's
# ideas and the pressure. Ideas of the focus tree itself are added to the list when they are created (phase 3).
VIE_hl_cleanup = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_pressure_tense_idea }
		remove_ideas = VIE_hl_pressure_tense_idea
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_pressure_crisis_idea }
		remove_ideas = VIE_hl_pressure_crisis_idea
	}
	# Ideas of the focus tree. The handover ending (X3) sets VIE_hl_keep_legacy and keeps two of them.
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_state_leading_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_state_leading_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_state_transition_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_state_transition_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_soe_spearhead_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_soe_spearhead_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_fdi_slowdown_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_fdi_slowdown_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_planning_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_planning_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_all_people_defence_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_all_people_defence_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_cyber_content_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_cyber_content_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_school_theory_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_school_theory_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_controlled_renewal_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_controlled_renewal_idea }

	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_self_reliance_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_self_reliance_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_investors_wary_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_investors_wary_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_defense_self_reliance_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_defense_self_reliance_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_indochina_union_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_indochina_union_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_unbreakable_fortress_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_unbreakable_fortress_idea }
	hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_hl_self_reliance_running }
	hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_hl_self_reliance_extended }
	# One-time reaction events and tree markers: a later return to the hardline party starts the branch afresh
	hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_hl_tree_open }
	hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_hl_fusion_done }
	hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_hl_sovereignty_line }

	hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev4_done }
	hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_hl_ev6_done }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_ideology_work_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_ideology_work_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_hl_army_party_work_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_hl_army_party_work_idea }
	# Security ideas and flag cleanup
	hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = VIE_security_active }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_security_state_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_security_state_idea }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_cyber_control_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_cyber_control_idea }

	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_state_data_center_idea } remove_ideas = VIE_state_data_center_idea }
	clear_variable = VIE_rp
}

# ====================================================================
# SECTION: VIE_md_effects_historical_events.txt
# ====================================================================

# Len lich cac su kien lich su co moc ngay/condition ro rang; caller chay hang thang
# trong country scope co original_tag = VIE (VIE_md_on_actions.txt).
VIE_event_scheduler_historical_events = {
	# Bao Yagi: tick thang 09/2024 len lich popup sau 15 ngay, de focus Luat Phong thu dan su
	# (70 ngay, mo sau 30/06/2024) co the hoan thanh truoc khi nguoi choi chon phuong an.
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 2024.8.31
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_disaster_yagi }
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_disaster_yagi }
		country_event = { id = vie_disaster.1 days = 15 }
	}

	# Khung hoang xang dau 2022: thay MTTH 3 thang bang mot lich mot lan tu ngay moc.
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 2022.6.1
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_petro_crisis_handled }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_petro_crisis }
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_petro_crisis }
		country_event = { id = vie_petro.1 days = 90 }
	}

	# Lien minh xe dien: chi len lich sau khi ca hai focus da hoan tat.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIE_ind_auto_ev_done
			has_completed_focus = VIE_petrolimex_green_ev_hubs
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_green_mobility_alliance_active }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_green_mobility }
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_green_mobility }
		country_event = { id = vie_auto.2 days = 20 }
	}
}

# Luc quan v39: scope COUNTRY; reward mot lan, reconciliation khong cong lai bien.
VIE_lf_xp_10_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_selected_land_grand_doctrine = yes }
		add_mastery = { amount = 10 folder = land }
	}
	else = { army_experience = 10 }
}

	}
	else = { army_experience = 15 }
}

# --- NÂNG CẤP TRỤC T: TỔ CHỨC & CHỈ HUY (VPA_Naval_Org: 1 -> 2 -> 3 -> 4) ---

VIE_nav_upgrade_org = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VPA_Naval_Org_3 }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = VPA_Naval_Org_3
			add_idea = VPA_Naval_Org_4
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VPA_Naval_Org_2 }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = VPA_Naval_Org_2
			add_idea = VPA_Naval_Org_3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VPA_Naval_Org_1 }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = VPA_Naval_Org_1
			add_idea = VPA_Naval_Org_2
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_ideas = VPA_Naval_Org_1
	}
}

# --- NÂNG CẤP TRỤC S: NHẬN THỨC BIỂN MDA (VPA_Maritime_MDA: 1 -> 2 -> 3 -> 4) ---

# --- XỬ LÝ PHẦN THƯỞNG CAPSTONE TỐI THƯỢNG F02 ---

VIE_nav_grant_capstone_f2 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_nav_p_doctrine_completed }
		add_ideas = VIE_nav_capstone_iron_shield
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_nav_g_doctrine_completed }
		add_ideas = VIE_nav_capstone_master_of_seas
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_nav_b_doctrine_completed }
		add_ideas = VIE_nav_capstone_ocean_surge
	}
	else = {
		add_ideas = VIE_nav_capstone_master_of_seas
	}

	# Bonus tự hào công nghiệp đóng tàu nội địa nếu đã hoàn thành I04
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = VIE_nav_i04_domestic_corvette_class }
		add_ideas = VIE_nav_shipbuilding_mastery
	}
}

# Vietnam submod scripted effects (Phase 2): later-period scheduler, leader, silent fallbacks.
# Called from on_monthly_VIE (VIE_md_on_actions.txt) after VIE_event_scheduler.

# VIE_create_leader_to_lam lives in VIE_md_effects.txt (defined before VIE_recreate_general_secretary)
VIE_new_leader_to_lam = {
	hidden_effect = {
		kill_country_leader = yes
		set_country_flag = VIE_congress_controls_leader
		set_variable = { VIE_gs = 5 }
		VIE_create_leader_to_lam = yes
	}
}

########################################################################
# Silent historical outcomes for class B events whose pop-up window has passed
########################################################################
VIE_fb_none = {
	log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: VIE scheduler fallback (no effect)"
}
VIE_fb_pmu18 = {
	decrease_corruption = yes
}
VIE_fb_inflation = {
	decrease_economic_growth = yes
}
VIE_fb_vinashin = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_vinashin_crisis_processed } } if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_ind_ship_governance_done } set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.75 } } else = { set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -2 } } modify_treasury_effect = yes set_country_flag = VIE_vinashin_crisis_processed }
}
VIE_fb_bad_debt = {
	set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -2 }
	modify_treasury_effect = yes
}
VIE_fb_salinity = {
	set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -3 }
	change_farmers_opinion = yes
}
# The focus VIE_us_embargo_lifted needs this flag, so the flag is set even without the pop-up
VIE_fb_embargo = {
	hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_us_arms_open }
	add_opinion_modifier = { target = USA modifier = VIE_comprehensive_partnership }
	reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = USA modifier = VIE_comprehensive_partnership }
}
VIE_fb_formosa = {
	add_stability = -0.02
}
VIE_fb_viet_a = {
	decrease_corruption = yes
}
VIE_fb_vtp = {
	decrease_corruption = yes
}
VIE_fb_labor_code = {
	hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_labor_convention_ratified }
}

########################################################################
# Scheduler, part 2 (2003 onwards). Order = priority when the 45 day cooldown is contested.
########################################################################
VIE_event_scheduler_p2 = {
	# ---- Class A: turning points
	if = {
		limit = { VIE_party_rule_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				date > 2020.12.31
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_congress_13 }
			}

			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_yagi }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd } }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_yagi }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
			country_event = { id = vie_soc.7 days = 3 random_days = 20 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { date > 2025.1.31 }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_yagi }
			VIE_fb_none = yes
		}
	}

	# ---- Random central-Vietnam floods: 4 % per month, then at least ~2.7 years before the next one
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 2002.12.31
			NOT = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_flood_cd }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd }
		}
		random = {
			chance = 4
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_flood_cd value = 1 days = 1000 } }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
			country_event = { id = vie_soc.3 days = 3 random_days = 20 }
		}
	}
}
