# Vietnam submod scripted effects (Phase 3): doors, regime ticks, collapse and civil war.
# All of it is called from VIE_event_scheduler_p3 (monthly) or from focuses/events.

########################################################################
# Regime entry helper: idempotent wrapper around VIE_transition_regime with a popularity boost.
#   VIE_enter_regime = { PARTY = 20 AMOUNT = 0.20 }
########################################################################
########################################################################
# Doors: a conditional event that fires once; the event's own options gate the alternatives.
########################################################################
########################################################################
# Re-creates the "already happened" flags in catch-up mode (VIE_catch_up = 1). Not called by any hook now; kept for later use.
# The winner may be the old country or the rebel country, so it re-creates the "already happened" flags.
########################################################################
VIE_catch_up_schedule = {
	set_temp_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 }
	VIE_event_scheduler = yes
	VIE_event_scheduler_p2 = yes
	VIE_event_scheduler_p3 = yes
	VIE_event_scheduler_p5 = yes
	VIE_event_scheduler_p6 = yes
	VIE_event_scheduler_p7 = yes
	VIE_event_scheduler_p10 = yes
	VIE_event_scheduler_p13 = yes
}

########################################################################
# Silent fallbacks / small helpers used by regime events
########################################################################
########################################################################
# Scheduler, part 3.  Called monthly after part 1 and part 2.  Skipped in catch-up mode.
########################################################################
VIE_event_scheduler_p3 = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } } }

		# ---- Doors (fire once each; options are gated inside the events)
		if = {
			limit = {
				VIE_party_rule_active = yes
				has_country_flag = VIE_hd981_done
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_regime_changed_recently }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_door_d4_done } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_door_d4_done }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
				country_event = { id = vie_alt.1 days = 20 random_days = 20 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				VIE_party_rule_active = yes
				date > 2006.10.31
				has_country_flag = VIE_press_relaxed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_door_d2_done } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_door_d2_done }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
				country_event = { id = vie_alt.2 days = 20 random_days = 20 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				VIE_party_rule_active = yes
				date > 2011.5.31
				OR = {
					has_stability < 0.55
					check_variable = { VIE_vinashin_risk > 1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_door_d3_done } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_door_d3_done }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
				country_event = { id = vie_alt.3 days = 20 random_days = 20 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				VIE_party_rule_active = yes
				date > 2018.5.31
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_door_d6_done } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_door_d6_done }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
				country_event = { id = vie_alt.6 days = 20 random_days = 20 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				VIE_party_rule_active = yes
				date > 2022.9.30
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_door_d8_done } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_door_d8_done }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
				country_event = { id = vie_alt.7 days = 20 random_days = 20 }
			}
		}

	}
}

# SECTION: VIE_md_effects_p11.txt
# ====================================================================

# Scheduler, part 11 (plan v6 batch 7): the last conditional events. Skipped in catch-up mode.
VIE_event_scheduler_p11 = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } } }

		# Door D7: a legitimacy crisis after the pandemic years, if stability is low
		if = {
			limit = {
				VIE_party_rule_active = yes
				date > 2021.8.31
				has_stability < 0.35
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_door_d7_done } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_door_d7_done }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
				country_event = { id = vie_alt.23 days = 20 random_days = 20 }
			}
		}

		# South China Sea: level 3 (a clash at the Paracels), once
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { VIE_scs_tension = 3 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_paracel_clash_seen }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_paracel_clash_seen }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
			country_event = { id = vie_scs.16 days = 3 }
		}

		# ...and ceasefire talks once that war has run for four months
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_war_with = CHI
				has_country_flag = VIE_paracel_clash
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_war_clock }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_truce_talks_done }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_truce_talks_done }
			country_event = { id = vie_scs.17 days = 3 }
		}

	}
}

# Scheduler, part 13 (Congress spine Phase 2, see VIE_congress_spine_redesign.md section 9).
# Hand-written, unlike VIE_md_effects_p10.txt (generated by gen_ev6.py): safe to edit.
# Called from on_monthly (VIE_md_on_actions.txt) and from VIE_catch_up_schedule (VIE_md_effects_p3.txt).

########################################################################
# Silent fallback for vie_pol.29 if the pop-up window closes unseen: applies the
# historical option (a) - acting President, then General Secretary elected concurrently
# as President.
########################################################################
VIE_fb_president_2018 = {
	add_political_power = 50
	hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_dual_role_2018 }
}

VIE_event_scheduler_p13 = {
	# vie_pol.28 (2013 Constitution): class A, regime-independent. The
	# VIE_constitution_2013_adopted flag is set even in catch-up mode, because it gates
	# the later "Implement the 2013 Constitution" focus for every regime (see section
	# 9.1 test 5); only the pop-up itself is skipped for catch-up.
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 2013.11.27
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_constitution2013 }
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_constitution2013 }
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_constitution_2013_adopted }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } } }
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
			country_event = { id = vie_pol.28 days = 3 random_days = 15 }
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = { VIE_party_rule_active = yes }

		# vie_pol.29 (President dies in office): only on the historical GS path
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_trong_third_term }
			if = {
				limit = {
					date > 2018.9.20
					NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_president2018 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } }
					hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_president2018 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd } }
					hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_president2018 }
					hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
					country_event = { id = vie_pol.29 days = 3 random_days = 15 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { date > 2019.3.31 }
					hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_president2018 }
					VIE_fb_president_2018 = yes
				}
			}
		}

		# Sixteenth National Assembly election (15 Mar 2026): reuses vie_pol.14
		# (see VIE_md_p10.txt), same class B pattern as its 2002/07/11/16/21 iterations
		if = {
			limit = {
				date > 2026.3.14
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_sched_na26 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { VIE_catch_up = 1 } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_na26 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_popup_cd } }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_na26 }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = VIE_popup_cd value = 1 days = 45 } }
				country_event = { id = vie_pol.14 days = 3 random_days = 20 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { date > 2026.9.30 }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_sched_na26 }
				VIE_fb_none = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# SECTION: VIE_md_effects_p16.txt
# ====================================================================

VIE_event_scheduler_p16 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 2014.12.31
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_md_roster_recruited_2015 }
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_md_roster_event_pending }
		country_event = { id = vie_army_commanders.2 days = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 2025.12.31
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_army_expansion_recruited }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_army_expansion_event_pending }
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = VIE_army_expansion_event_pending }
		country_event = { id = vie_army_commanders.1 days = 1 }
	}
}
